# Seed 45 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - not small: hypervolume +6.2%, the memory landscape moved (Iteration 240)

`seed 45 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit dbdf4cb8 (Iteration 230: the after-seed-44 batch - native
WRITE-LINE gives back its read-ahead, file-buffer slots apart, READ's FS-SEEK0
gone, the loader's 32 KB local static, a zero divisor -10 THROW), started
20:46; the evolution 20:49-00:40 (3 h 51 min, generations 0-84), the front
measured again by 00:52, the comparison packed by 02:12. 21,524 designs made,
17,797 measured (17,682 alive), 3,727 screened - not measured.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest +0.9%, smallest +0.54%,
memory +4 KB, **hypervolume +6.2%**, best Q -1.6% - **not small**: the stopping
rule counts afresh. The Q-best design of all runs is seed 44's cc678c891d: 0.204
at 26,108 bytes, 168 KB (Q 0.273 of s6's - s6's own memory moved with the
batch). Calibration s6/s6 0.990; cpu 2 against cpu 8 median 1.003, Spearman
1.00. Seed 44 was small; seed 45, on the same genes with the memory fixes, is
not: the file buffers' slots apart made 4 KB buffers as cheap in memory as
small ones, so designs earlier runs had dropped as memory-dominated are on the
front now - the reason Iteration 230 named if it were not small.

From the run's database (Iteration 240):

- Deaths: 115, every one "reach limit at scale 0 - not run"; none on the corpus.
- The screen: 3,727 of the 19,918 children from generation 5 skipped (18.7%).
- Children by the try that made them: mutation 57.3%, crossover 31.5%, borrowed
  11.2% (the last fifth: crossover 32.2%).
- Via the attic: 3,084 children; 2 of the 105 on the final front.
- The final front, 105 designs: mutation 77, carried 12, borrowed 8, crossover 8.
- fsys: 2 on 53, 3 on 27, 5 on 11, 4 on 7, 0 on 7; the smallest tenth all fsys
  3, the fast third all fsys 2.
- fbuf (designs with native file words): 4 KB buffers on 46, 1 KB on 14 - the
  other way round from seed 44 (23 and 49): with the slots apart, full buffers
  cost no memory and save reads.
- threads: 16 chains on 85 of the 105, the dump's 32 on 20.
- Memory on the front 124-188 KB (seed 44 recorded 160-224): the batch's two
  memory fixes, on every design.
- In the run: fastest 26be0b1ed2 0.167 at 30,753; smallest c1cc8e127c 16,296
  (speed 1.434) - the batch's zero-divisor THROW in every engine and image.

---

# Evolved VM designs

17797 designs evaluated, 17682 alive, 3727 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 2393d3e5fd, speed 0.176, binary + image 26,723, memory 184 KB - Q 0.262 of s6-cv8b's.
